  80: const PROVISIONAL_INSIGHT_LOW_STEP = 0.03;
  81: const PROVISIONAL_INSIGHT_DEFAULT_STEP = 0.05;
  82: const PROVISIONAL_LEARNING_SCORE_HIGH_CONFUSION_STEP = 0.01;
  83: const PROVISIONAL_LEARNING_SCORE_DEFAULT_STEP = 0.04;
  84: 
  85: function isFiniteNumber(value: unknown): value is number {
  86:   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value);
  87: }
  88: 
  89: function isUsableModelSignalValue(value: unknown): value is number {
  90:   return isFiniteNumber(value) && value >= 0 && value <= 1;
  91: }
  92: 
  93: type UsableModelSignals = ModelSignals & {
  94:   model_confusion: number;
  95:   model_insight: number;
  96: };
  97: 
  98: function hasUsableModelSignals(
  99:   modelSignals?: ModelSignals,
 100: ): modelSignals is UsableModelSignals {
 101:   if (!modelSignals) return false;
 102: 
 103:   return (
 104:     modelSignals.status === "ok" &&
 105:     isUsableModelSignalValue(modelSignals.model_confusion) &&
 106:     isUsableModelSignalValue(modelSignals.model_insight)
 107:   );
 108: }
 109: 
 110: function getModelConfusion(modelSignals?: ModelSignals) {
 111:   if (!hasUsableModelSignals(modelSignals)) return null;
 112: 
 113:   return modelSignals.model_confusion;
 114: }
 115: 
 116: function getModelInsight(modelSignals?: ModelSignals) {
 117:   if (!hasUsableModelSignals(modelSignals)) return null;
 118: 
 119:   return modelSignals.model_insight;
 120: }

 122: function getPrimaryMessageFrameCacheKey(message: string) {
 123:   return message.trim().toLowerCase();
 124: }
 125: 
 126: function getCachedPrimaryMessageFrame(message: string): PrimaryMessageFrame {
 127:   const key = getPrimaryMessageFrameCacheKey(message);
 128: 
 129:   if (!key) {
 130:     return inferPrimaryMessageFrame(message);
 131:   }
 132: 
 133:   const cached = primaryMessageFrameCache.get(key);
 134: 
 135:   if (cached) {
 136:     return cached;
 137:   }
 138: 
 139:   const frame = inferPrimaryMessageFrame(message);
 140:   primaryMessageFrameCache.set(key, frame);
 141: 
 142:   if (primaryMessageFrameCache.size > PRIMARY_MESSAGE_FRAME_CACHE_LIMIT) {
 143:     const oldestKey = primaryMessageFrameCache.keys().next().value;
 144: 
 145:     if (oldestKey) {
 146:       primaryMessageFrameCache.delete(oldestKey);
 147:     }
 148:   }
 149: 
 150:   return frame;
 151: }

 193: export function inferPreferredModality(
 194:   message: string,
 195:   modeHint?: "clarify" | "probe"
 196: ): RendererModality {
 197:   const frame = getCachedPrimaryMessageFrame(message);

  53: function selectFollowupDiagnosis(args: {
  54:   classification: AttemptClassification;
  55:   activeDiagnosis: DiagnosisType;
  56:   misconceptionTags: string[];
  57: }) {
  58:   const { classification, activeDiagnosis, misconceptionTags } = args;
  59: 
  60:   if (classification === "success") {
  61:     return "transfer_gap" as DiagnosisType;
  62:   }

  64:   if (classification === "no_response") {
  65:     return "representation_gap" as DiagnosisType;
  66:   }
  67: 
  68:   if (misconceptionTags.includes("failed_transfer_mapping")) {
  69:     return "transfer_gap" as DiagnosisType;
  70:   }
  71: 
  72:   if (
  73:     misconceptionTags.includes("weak_structural_representation") ||
  74:     misconceptionTags.includes("missing_mechanism")
  75:   ) {
  76:     return "representation_gap" as DiagnosisType;
  77:   }
  78: 
  79:   if (misconceptionTags.includes("weak_topic_grounding")) {
  80:     return "recall_gap" as DiagnosisType;
  81:   }
  82: 
  83:   return activeDiagnosis;
  84: }
  85: 
  86: function selectProbeTypeForDiagnosis(
  87:   diagnosis: DiagnosisType,
  88:   classification: AttemptClassification
  89: ): ProbeType {
  90:   if (classification === "success") {
  91:     return "apply_transfer";
  92:   }
  93: 
  94:   switch (diagnosis) {
  95:     case "recall_gap":
  96:       return "predict";
  97:     case "representation_gap":
  98:       return "explain";
  99:     case "procedure_gap":
 100:       return "transform";
 101:     case "discrimination_gap":
 102:       return "discriminate";
 103:     case "transfer_gap":
 104:       return "apply_transfer";
 105:     default:
 106:       return "explain";
 107:   }
 108: }

 110: function buildPromptForProbe(args: {
 111:   topic: RouteTopic;
 112:   probeType: ProbeType;
 113:   classification: AttemptClassification;
 114:   missingElements?: string | null;
 115: }) {
 116:   const { topic, probeType, classification, missingElements } = args;
 117:   const missingSuffix = missingElements
 118:     ? ` Focus especially on this missing piece: ${missingElements}`
 119:     : "";
 120: 
 121:   switch (probeType) {
 122:     case "predict":
 123:       return `Without just repeating a definition, predict what would happen in a simple case involving ${topic.topic_label}, and explain why.${missingSuffix}`;
 124: 
 125:     case "discriminate":
 126:       return `What is the key difference that helps distinguish ${topic.topic_label} from a closely related idea? Explain the difference clearly.${missingSuffix}`;
 127: 
 128:     case "transform":
 129:       return `Walk through ${topic.topic_label} as a step-by-step process. What happens first, what happens next, and why does the order matter?${missingSuffix}`;
 130: 
 131:     case "apply_transfer":
 132:       return classification === "success"
 133:         ? `Now apply ${topic.topic_label} in a slightly different situation. What changes, what stays the same, and why?${missingSuffix}`
 134:         : `Apply ${topic.topic_label} in a new but related situation. Show how the same core idea still works.${missingSuffix}`;
 135: 
 136:     case "explain":
 137:     default:
 138:       return `Can you explain ${topic.topic_label} in a more concrete way, using a specific example or clear cause-and-effect chain?${missingSuffix}`;
 139:   }
 140: }
 141: 
 142: function buildTitleForProbe(topic: RouteTopic, probeType: ProbeType) {
 143:   switch (probeType) {
 144:     case "predict":
 145:       return `Predict what happens in ${topic.topic_label}`;
 146:     case "discriminate":
 147:       return `Distinguish ${topic.topic_label} clearly`;
 148:     case "transform":
 149:       return `Walk through ${topic.topic_label} step by step`;
 150:     case "apply_transfer":
 151:       return `Apply ${topic.topic_label} in a new situation`;
 152:     case "explain":
 153:     default:
 154:       return `Explain ${topic.topic_label} more concretely`;
 155:   }
 156: }

  81: function tokenizeNormalized(text: string) {

  88: function normalizeSemanticToken(token: string) {
  89:   if (token.length <= 3) return token;
  90:   if (token.endsWith("ies") && token.length > 4) {
  91:     return `${token.slice(0, -3)}y`;
  92:   }
  93:   if (token.endsWith("ses") || token.endsWith("xes")) {
  94:     return token.slice(0, -2);
  95:   }
  96:   if (token.endsWith("s") && !token.endsWith("ss")) {
  97:     return token.slice(0, -1);
  98:   }
  99:   return token;
 100: }

 103:   return Array.from(

 245: function inferMisconceptionTags(args: {
 246:   classification: ResponseScoring["classification"];

 248:   conceptCoverage: number;
 249:   causalChainScore: number;

 270:   }
 271: 
 272:   if (args.causalChainScore < 0.22 && args.wordCount >= 8) {
 273:     tags.add("missing_mechanism");
 274:   }
 275: 
 276:   if (
 277:     args.activeDiagnosis === "transfer_gap" &&
 278:     args.transferSignalScore < 0.18 &&
 279:     args.wordCount >= 12
 280:   ) {
 281:     tags.add("weak_generalization");
 282:   }
 283: 
 284:   if (args.uncertaintyHits >= 2) {
 285:     tags.add("unstable_confidence");
 286:   }
 287: 
 288:   return Array.from(tags);
 289: }

  29: export function asEmbeddingVector(value: unknown): EmbeddingVector | null {
  30:   if (!Array.isArray(value)) return null;
  31: 
  32:   const vector = value.filter(
  33:     (item): item is number => typeof item === "number" && Number.isFinite(item),
  34:   );
  35: 
  36:   if (!vector.length) return null;
  37:   if (vector.length !== value.length) return null;
  38: 
  39:   return vector;
  40: }
  41: 
  42: export function asPositiveCount(value: number | null | undefined) {
  43:   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0
  44:     ? Math.floor(value)
  45:     : null;
  46: }

 100: function isEmptyRawResponse(value: ProbeAttemptPayload["response"]) {
 101:   if (value === null || value === undefined) return true;
 102: 
 103:   if (typeof value === "string") {
 104:     return value.trim().length === 0;
 105:   }
 106: 
 107:   if (typeof value === "object") {
 108:     return Object.keys(value).length === 0;
 109:   }
 110: 
 111:   return false;
 112: }

 114: function normalizeRawAttemptResponse(value: ProbeAttemptPayload["response"]) {
 115:   return typeof value === "string" || (value && typeof value === "object")
 116:     ? value
 117:     : null;

 120: function expectedResponseTypeFromAttempt(
 121:   value: ImportantRunInputs["new_attempt"]["response_type"],
 122: ): ProbeExpectedResponseType | null {
 123:   switch (value) {
 124:     case "choice":
 125:     case "multiple_choice":
 126:     case "ordering":
 127:     case "predict":
 128:     case "audio":
 129:     case "video":
 130:     case "interactive_action":
 131:     case "dynamic_task":
 132:     case "text":
 133:       return value;
 134:     case "classify":
 135:       /**
 136:        * There is no separate ProbeExpectedResponseType branch for classify in
 137:        * the current contracts. Classification is treated as structured /
 138:        * interactive evidence downstream.
 139:        */
 140:       return "interactive_action";
 141:     case "transform":
 142:       return "text";
 143:     default:
 144:       return null;
 145:   }
 146: }
 147: 
 148: function inferredProbeTypeFromResponseType(
 149:   value: ImportantRunInputs["new_attempt"]["response_type"],
 150: ): ProbeType | null {
 151:   switch (value) {
 152:     case "predict":
 153:       return "predict";
 154:     case "choice":
 155:     case "multiple_choice":
 156:     case "classify":
 157:       return "discriminate";
 158:     case "ordering":
 159:     case "interactive_action":
 160:     case "dynamic_task":
 161:     case "transform":
 162:       return "transform";
 163:     case "text":
 164:     case "audio":
 165:     case "video":
 166:     default:
 167:       return null;
 168:   }
 169: }

  31:  *
  32:  * This file does not perform the core contract judgment itself. It consumes

  36:  * The important V1.1 change:

  55: function clamp01(value: number) {
  56:   if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;
  57:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value));
  58: }
  59: 
  60: export function buildPreviousModeOutcome(): PreviousModeOutcome {
  61:   return {
  62:     mode_selected: "probe",
  63:     reasons: ["This run follows a submitted probe response."],
  64:     confidence: 0.82,
  65:     clarify_outcome: "not_applicable",
  66:   };
  67: }
  68: 
  69: function bodyResponseSignal(scoring: ReturnType<typeof scoreResponse>) {
  70:   if (scoring.classification === "no_response") return 0.05;
  71:   if (scoring.classification === "guess") return 0.25;
  72:   if (scoring.classification === "structural_failure") return 0.35;
  73:   if (scoring.classification === "near_miss") return 0.6;
  74:   if (scoring.classification === "success") return 0.85;
  75:   return 0.4;
  76: }
  77: 
  78: function blendSignals(args: {
  79:   current: number;
  80:   interpretationSignal?: number | null;
  81:   interpretationWeight?: number;
  82: }) {
  83:   const signal =
  84:     typeof args.interpretationSignal === "number" &&
  85:     Number.isFinite(args.interpretationSignal)
  86:       ? clamp01(args.interpretationSignal)
  87:       : null;
  88: 
  89:   if (signal === null) return clamp01(args.current);
  90: 
  91:   const weight = clamp01(args.interpretationWeight ?? 0.22);
  92:   return clamp01(args.current * (1 - weight) + signal * weight);
  93: }
  94: 
  95: function buildInterpretationDecisionReasons(
  96:   attemptInterpretation: AttemptInterpretation | null | undefined,
  97: ) {
  98:   if (!attemptInterpretation) return [];
  99: 
 100:   const reasons = [
 101:     `Engine evidence interpreted the attempt as ${attemptInterpretation.modality} evidence with outcome ${attemptInterpretation.outcome}.`,
 102:     `Engine evidence strength was ${attemptInterpretation.evidence_strength.toFixed(
 103:       2,
 104:     )} with interpretation confidence ${attemptInterpretation.judgment_confidence.toFixed(
 105:       2,
 106:     )}.`,
 107:   ];
 108: 
 109:   if (attemptInterpretation.cautions.length > 0) {
 110:     reasons.push(`Evidence interpretation caution: ${attemptInterpretation.cautions[0]}`);
 111:   }
 112: 
 113:   return reasons;
 114: }
 115: 
 116: function buildContractJudgmentDecisionReasons(
 117:   contractJudgment: ContractJudgment | null | undefined,
 118: ) {
 119:   if (!contractJudgment) return [];
 120: 
 121:   const reasons = [
 122:     `Contract judgment outcome was ${contractJudgment.outcome}.`,
 123:     `Contract evidence tier was ${contractJudgment.evidence_tier}.`,
 124:     `Contract success score was ${contractJudgment.success_score.toFixed(
 125:       2,
 126:     )} and failure score was ${contractJudgment.failure_score.toFixed(2)}.`,
 127:     `Contract confidence was ${contractJudgment.contract_confidence.toFixed(2)}.`,
 128:   ];
 129: 
 130:   if (contractJudgment.structured_judgment) {
 131:     reasons.push(
 132:       `Structured judgment was ${contractJudgment.structured_judgment.outcome} with performance ${contractJudgment.structured_judgment.performance_score.toFixed(
 133:         2,
 134:       )}.`,
 135:     );
 136:   }
 137: 
 138:   if (contractJudgment.cautions.length > 0) {
 139:     reasons.push(`Contract judgment caution: ${contractJudgment.cautions[0]}`);
 140:   }
 141: 
 142:   return reasons;
 143: }
 144: 
 145: function contractReadinessSignal(
 146:   contractJudgment: ContractJudgment | null | undefined,
 147: ) {
 148:   if (!contractJudgment) return null;
 149: 
 150:   return clamp01(
 151:     contractJudgment.success_score * 0.48 +
 152:       (1 - contractJudgment.failure_score) * 0.2 +
 153:       contractJudgment.contract_confidence * 0.22 +
 154:       contractJudgment.evidence_strength * 0.1,
 155:   );
 156: }
 157: 
 158: function contractEvidenceQualitySignal(
 159:   contractJudgment: ContractJudgment | null | undefined,
 160: ) {
 161:   if (!contractJudgment) return null;
 162: 
 163:   const structuredBoost =
 164:     contractJudgment.structured_judgment &&
 165:     contractJudgment.structured_judgment.outcome !== "not_applicable" &&
 166:     contractJudgment.structured_judgment.outcome !== "unjudgeable"
 167:       ? contractJudgment.structured_judgment.confidence * 0.12
 168:       : 0;
 169: 
 170:   return clamp01(
 171:     contractJudgment.evidence_strength * 0.34 +
 172:       contractJudgment.contract_confidence * 0.32 +
 173:       Math.abs(contractJudgment.success_score - contractJudgment.failure_score) * 0.22 +
 174:       structuredBoost,
 175:   );
 176: }
 177: 
 178: function contractDecisionConfidenceBoost(
 179:   contractJudgment: ContractJudgment | null | undefined,
 180: ) {
 181:   if (!contractJudgment) return 0;
 182: 
 183:   const outcomeBoost =
 184:     contractJudgment.outcome === "contract_success"
 185:       ? 0.05
 186:       : contractJudgment.outcome === "contract_failure"
 187:         ? 0.04
 188:         : contractJudgment.outcome === "contract_partial"
 189:           ? 0.025
 190:           : 0;
 191: 
 192:   return clamp01(contractJudgment.contract_confidence * 0.06 + outcomeBoost);
 193: }
 194: 
 195: export function buildDecision(args: {
 196:   topic: RouteTopic;
 197:   scoring: ReturnType<typeof scoreResponse>;
 198:   replyBundle: ReturnType<typeof buildResponseBundle>;
 199:   modelSignals: ModelSignals;
 200:   attemptInterpretation?: AttemptInterpretation | null;
 201:   contractJudgment?: ContractJudgment | null;
 202: }): InterventionModeDecision {
 203:   const {
 204:     topic,
 205:     scoring,
 206:     replyBundle,
 207:     modelSignals,
 208:     attemptInterpretation,
 209:     contractJudgment,
 210:   } = args;
 211:   const continueWithProbe = replyBundle.nextMode === "probe";
 212: 
 213:   const usableModelSignals = hasUsableModelSignals(modelSignals);
 214:   const confusion = usableModelSignals ? modelSignals.model_confusion : null;
 215:   const insight = usableModelSignals ? modelSignals.model_insight : null;
 216: 
 217:   const baseReadinessSignal =
 218:     insight !== null
 219:       ? Math.max(
 220:           0,
 221:           Math.min(
 222:             1,
 223:             scoring.correctnessEstimate * 0.42 +
 224:               scoring.evidenceStrength * 0.28 +
 225:               insight * 0.3,
 226:           ),
 227:         )
 228:       : Math.max(
 229:           0,
 230:           Math.min(
 231:             1,
 232:             scoring.correctnessEstimate * 0.6 + scoring.evidenceStrength * 0.4,
 233:           ),
 234:         );
 235: 
 236:   const readinessWithInterpretation = blendSignals({
 237:     current: baseReadinessSignal,
 238:     interpretationSignal:
 239:       attemptInterpretation === null || attemptInterpretation === undefined
 240:         ? null
 241:         : attemptInterpretation.evidence_strength * 0.56 +
 242:           attemptInterpretation.judgment_confidence * 0.44,
 243:     interpretationWeight: 0.18,
 244:   });
 245: 
 246:   const readinessSignal = blendSignals({
 247:     current: readinessWithInterpretation,
 248:     interpretationSignal: contractReadinessSignal(contractJudgment),
 249:     interpretationWeight: 0.22,
 250:   });
 251: 
 252:   const baseEvidenceQualitySignal =
 253:     confusion !== null && insight !== null
 254:       ? Math.max(
 255:           0,
 256:           Math.min(
 257:             1,
 258:             scoring.explanationQuality * 0.34 +
 259:               scoring.evidenceStrength * 0.26 +
 260:               scoring.judgmentConfidence * 0.2 +
 261:               insight * 0.18 -
 262:               confusion * 0.12,
 263:           ),
 264:         )
 265:       : Math.max(
 266:           0,
 267:           Math.min(
 268:             1,
 269:             scoring.explanationQuality * 0.45 +
 270:               scoring.evidenceStrength * 0.35 +
 271:               scoring.judgmentConfidence * 0.2,
 272:           ),
 273:         );
 274: 
 275:   const evidenceQualityWithInterpretation = blendSignals({
 276:     current: baseEvidenceQualitySignal,
 277:     interpretationSignal:
 278:       attemptInterpretation === null || attemptInterpretation === undefined
 279:         ? null
 280:         : attemptInterpretation.evidence_strength,
 281:     interpretationWeight: 0.24,
 282:   });
 283: 
 284:   const evidenceQualitySignal = blendSignals({
 285:     current: evidenceQualityWithInterpretation,
 286:     interpretationSignal: contractEvidenceQualitySignal(contractJudgment),
 287:     interpretationWeight: 0.24,
 288:   });
 289: 
 290:   const classificationBase =
 291:     scoring.classification === "success"
 292:       ? 0.82
 293:       : scoring.classification === "near_miss"
 294:         ? 0.68
 295:         : scoring.classification === "structural_failure"
 296:           ? 0.64
 297:           : scoring.classification === "guess"
 298:             ? 0.58
 299:             : 0.54;
 300: 
 301:   const decisionConfidence = Math.max(
 302:     0,
 303:     Math.min(
 304:       0.95,
 305:       classificationBase +
 306:         scoring.evidenceStrength * 0.1 +
 307:         scoring.judgmentConfidence * 0.12 +
 308:         (attemptInterpretation?.judgment_confidence ?? 0) * 0.04 +
 309:         contractDecisionConfidenceBoost(contractJudgment) +
 310:         (insight !== null ? insight * 0.04 : 0) -
 311:         (confusion !== null ? confusion * 0.03 : 0),
 312:     ),
 313:   );
 314: 
 315:   const decisionReasons = [
 316:     "This run is directly downstream of a delivered probe.",
 317:     `The judged attempt classification was ${scoring.classification}.`,
 318:     `Evidence strength was ${scoring.evidenceStrength.toFixed(
 319:       2,
 320:     )} and judgment confidence was ${scoring.judgmentConfidence.toFixed(2)}.`,

... truncated after 320 of 455 lines ...

  48:   }
  49: 
  50:   return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as ProbeContractSnapshot;
  51: }
  52: 
  53: function asActiveDiagnosis(value: unknown) {
  54:   return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : null;
  55: }
  56: 
  57: function getPersistedActiveDiagnosisFromTopicJson(topicJson: JsonValue) {
  58:   const topicJsonRecord = asJsonRecord(topicJson);
  59:   const diagnosisState = asJsonRecord(topicJsonRecord.diagnosis_state);
  60:   const stateActiveDiagnosis = asActiveDiagnosis(diagnosisState.active_diagnosis);
  61: 
  62:   if (stateActiveDiagnosis) return stateActiveDiagnosis;
  63: 
  64:   return asActiveDiagnosis(topicJsonRecord.active_diagnosis);
  65: }
  66: 
  67: function buildProbeContractPersistenceAudit(args: {
  68:   nextProbePlan:
  69:     | ReturnType<typeof buildNextProbePlan>
  70:     | ReturnType<typeof buildNotApplicableProbePlan>;
  71: }) {
  72:   const snapshot = asProbeContractSnapshot(
  73:     args.nextProbePlan.probe_contract_snapshot,
  74:   );
  75: 
  76:   if (!snapshot) {
  77:     return {
  78:       available: false,
  79:       contract_id: null,
  80:       version: null,
  81:       renderer_kind: null,
  82:       target_diagnosis: args.nextProbePlan.target_diagnosis ?? null,
  83:       probe_type: args.nextProbePlan.probe_type ?? null,
  84:       reason: "No applicable probe contract snapshot was attached to this probe plan.",
  85:     };
  86:   }
  87: 
  88:   return {
  89:     available: true,
  90:     contract_id: snapshot.contract_id ?? null,
  91:     version: snapshot.version ?? null,
  92:     renderer_kind: snapshot.renderer_kind ?? null,
  93:     target_diagnosis:
  94:       snapshot.target_diagnosis ?? args.nextProbePlan.target_diagnosis ?? null,
  95:     probe_type: snapshot.probe_type ?? args.nextProbePlan.probe_type ?? null,
  96:     assessment_target: snapshot.assessment_target ?? null,
  97:     success_marker_count: Array.isArray(
  98:       snapshot.judging_schema?.success_markers,
  99:     )
 100:       ? snapshot.judging_schema.success_markers.length
 101:       : null,
 102:     failure_marker_count: Array.isArray(
 103:       snapshot.judging_schema?.failure_markers,
 104:     )
 105:       ? snapshot.judging_schema.failure_markers.length
 106:       : null,
 107:     updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
 108:     source: "probe_plan_probe_contract_v1_1",
 109:   };
 110: }

 170: export function buildProbeSubmitTopicJson(args: {
 171:   topic: RouteTopic;
 172:   topicLabel: string;
 173:   pendingConfusionInsightScore: PendingConfusionInsightScore;
 174:   bodyProbeId: string;
 175:   judgedAttempt: ReturnType<typeof buildJudgedAttempt>;
 176:   attemptInterpretation: AttemptInterpretation;
 177:   contractJudgment: ContractJudgment;
 178:   answeredProbeContractSnapshot: ProbeContractSnapshot | null;
 179:   updatedTopicMetrics: ReturnType<typeof buildTopicMetricUpdate>;
 180:   modelSignals: ModelSignals;
 181:   nextProbePlan:
 182:     | ReturnType<typeof buildNextProbePlan>
 183:     | ReturnType<typeof buildNotApplicableProbePlan>;
 184:   nextDeliveredProbe: DeliveredProbe | null;

 186:   updatedPersistedTopic: RouteTopic;
 187: }): JsonValue {
 188:   const embeddingFields = getEmbeddingPersistenceFields(args.updatedPersistedTopic);
 189:

 191:     topicJson: args.topic.topic_json,
 192:     pendingScore: args.pendingConfusionInsightScore,
 193:   });
 194: 
 195:   const previousTopicJson = asJsonRecord(topicJsonWithPendingScore);
 196: 
 197:   const diagnosisStateUpdate = updateDiagnosisBeliefs({
 198:     previousState: previousTopicJson.diagnosis_state,
 199:     currentActiveDiagnosis:
 200:       args.contractJudgment.suggested_active_diagnosis ??
 201:       args.nextProbePlan.target_diagnosis ??
 202:       null,
 203:     attemptInterpretation: args.attemptInterpretation,
 204:     contractJudgment: args.contractJudgment,
 205:     source: "contract_judgment_v1_1",
 206:   });
 207: 
 208:   const persistedActiveDiagnosis = diagnosisStateUpdate.active_diagnosis;
 209: 
 210:   const answeredProbeContract = asProbeContractSnapshot(
 211:     args.answeredProbeContractSnapshot,
 212:   );
 213: 
 214:   const nextProbeContract = asProbeContractSnapshot(
 215:     args.nextProbePlan.probe_contract_snapshot,
 216:   );
 217:   const deliveredProbeContract = asProbeContractSnapshot(
 218:     args.nextDeliveredProbe?.probe_contract_snapshot ?? null,
 219:   );
 220: 
 221:   const probeContractAudit = buildProbeContractPersistenceAudit({
 222:     nextProbePlan: args.nextProbePlan,
 223:   });
 224: 
 225:   return JSON.parse(
 226:     JSON.stringify({
 227:       ...topicJsonWithPendingScore,
 228:       topic_id: args.topic.id,
 229:       topic_label: args.topicLabel,
 230: 
 231:       /**
 232:        * Keep top-level JSON diagnosis fields aligned with diagnosis_state so

 234:        */
 235:       active_diagnosis: persistedActiveDiagnosis,
 236:       diagnosis: persistedActiveDiagnosis,

 243:       engine_evidence_interpretation: buildEngineEvidenceInterpretationAudit({
 244:         attemptInterpretation: args.attemptInterpretation,
 245:       }),
 246:       last_engine_evidence_interpretation: {
 247:         ...buildEngineEvidenceInterpretationAudit({
 248:           attemptInterpretation: args.attemptInterpretation,
 249:         }),
 250:         updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
 251:         source: "probe_submit_engine_evidence_v1_1",
 252:       },

 256:       }),
